Q: An Austrian-British psychoanalyst born in 1882 and innovative in object relations theory was also rigorous about what other type of therapy?
A: "An Austrian-British psychoanalyst born in 1882 and innovative in object relations theory was Melanie Klein. While the information provided does not explicitly state that Melanie Klein was rigorous about any specific type of therapy beyond her work in object relations theory, her influence extended to the development of schema therapy.
